This explicit supervision enables models to learn layered video representations directly rather than inferring them implicitly. Building on this dataset, we propose DBL-Diffusion, a dual-branch diffusion framework that jointly models RGB composites and RGBA foreground layers through shared denoising and cross-branch interaction. We instantiate the framework in two tasks: DBL-Insert for layered object insertion, which generates explicit RGBA layers for realistic compositing and flexible post-editing, and DBL-Decompose for video layer decomposition, which recovers foreground and background layers using triplet supervision. Experiments demonstrate that explicit layer modeling substantially improves both insertion fidelity and decomposition quality.

</details>


### [68] [Freq-RemoteVAR: Next-Frequency Autoregressive Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25815)
*Luqi Gong,Rui Xu,Yue Chen,Chao Li,Jingqi Hong,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Freq-RemoteVAR提出一种基于频率域的自回归框架，将遥感变化检测重构为结构化生成问题。通过频域分解与渐进式频率预测，结合频率感知的掩码标记策略和频率自回归变压器，实现从粗到细的变化信息生成。引入尺度对齐的RoPE交叉注意力模块以增强时空一致性，并设计变化质量控制模块提升鲁棒性。在多个数据集上均优于现有方法，尤其在复杂外观变化和噪声干扰下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感变化检测方法多采用单步密集预测，忽视了变化模式的固有频率特性，导致在复杂场景下性能受限。

Method: 提出频率自回归框架Freq-RemoteVAR，包括频率感知掩码标记、频率自回归变压器、尺度对齐的RoPE交叉注意力（SRCA）模块及变化质量控制模块，实现频域中渐进式变化生成。

Result: 在CDD、GZ-CD、LEVIR-CD等多个数据集上显著优于现有方法，尤其在复杂外观变化和噪声干扰场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: Freq-RemoteVAR通过频率域建模与渐进式生成机制，有效捕捉变化的长程依赖关系，提升了变化检测在复杂环境下的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection aims to identify land-cover changes from bi-temporal images. Most existing methods follow a one-shot dense prediction paradigm, directly regressing a change mask from fused features. However, such approaches overlook the intrinsic frequency characteristics of change patterns. We propose Freq-RemoteVAR, a frequency autoregressive framework that reformulates change detection as a structured generation problem in the frequency domain. Instead of predicting the change mask in a single step, we introduce a next-frequency prediction paradigm, where change information is progressively generated from coarse to fine. We design a frequency-aware mask tokenization strategy that decomposes change supervision into multi-frequency token targets via Fourier transformation and quantization. We develop a Frequency VAR Transformer, which performs causal autoregressive modeling over frequency tokens. The model starts from learned mask queries and progressively predicts frequency-level tokens conditioned on previously generated tokens and bi-temporal image features, effectively capturing long-range dependencies across frequency scales. We introduce Scale-Aligned RoPE Cross Attention (SRCA) module, which aligns frequency-domain mask queries with spatial-domain bi-temporal features under a unified coordinate system, enhancing spatial-frequency consistency during generation. We propose a Change-quality Control module that adaptively modulates the generation process through dynamic normalization, attention biasing, and spatial offset adjustment, thereby suppressing pseudo-change responses and improving robustness. Extensive experiments on CDD, GZ-CD, and LEVIR-CD demonstrate that Freq-RemoteVAR consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly in challenging scenarios with complex appearance variations and noisy disturbances.

</details>


### [69] [SepPrune:A Separator-based Pruning Framework for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25818)
*Yuchen Wang,Qihui Zhu,Yang Liu,Xiaoyan Sun,Siying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SepPrune是一种高效、无需训练、即插即用的视觉令牌剪枝方法，利用模态分隔符作为统一查询来排序和选择信息丰富的视觉令牌，显著降低计算成本，同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌剪枝方法依赖跨模态注意力，无法在预填充阶段前剪枝，或基于多样性估计，计算开销大。本文旨在提出一种更高效、低延迟的剪枝方法。

Method: 提出SepPrune方法，利用模态分隔符作为统一查询，通过其注意力得分对视觉令牌进行排序与筛选，复用LLM内置投影参数，无需架构修改。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，SepPrune在保留96.3%原始准确率的同时，移除了80.2%的视觉令牌，达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: SepPrune是一种高效且通用的视觉令牌剪枝方案，适用于现有MLLM，显著降低计算开销，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs), such as Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, generate large numbers of vision tokens for high-resolution inputs, leading to substantial computational cost. Existing vision token pruning methods either depend on cross-modal attention and cannot prune before the prefill stage, or rely on diversity estimation with high computational overhead. We observe that attention scores from both vision and text tokens peak at modality separator tokens, suggesting that these separators bridge the two modalities. Based on this observation, we propose SepPrune, an efficient, training-free, plug-and-play pruning method that uses the separator token as a unified query to rank and select informative vision tokens. SepPrune reuses the LLM's built-in projection parameters and requires no architectural changes. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that SepPrune achieves state-of-the-art performance, retaining 96.3% of the original accuracy while removing 80.2% of vision tokens.

</details>


### [70] [Food Image Segmentation with LLM-Derived Ingredient Labels and Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25820)
*Jui-Feng Chi,Wei-Ta Chu,Sheng-Long Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种即插即用的多模态模块（LIM-F和LIM-Q），利用大语言模型（LLM）从食物图像中推断出的食材标签，提升食物图像分割性能。LIM-F适用于多层输出的图像编码器（如Swin Transformer），LIM-Q针对Mask2Former类Transformer解码器。两者无需预先对齐图像与文本，可直接将语义食材信息注入视觉分析流程。在FoodSeg103基准上，该方法达到当前最优性能：Swin-L+Mask2Former搭配LIM-Q实现55.0 mIoU；LIM-F在相同模型下达54.4 mIoU，且在CNN架构中也实现47.7到49.8 mIoU的提升。训练时GPU内存增加最多仅3.8 GB，具备良好的实用性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物图像分割模型在视觉相似食材和罕见食物类别上表现不佳，缺乏有效利用语义信息的方法。为提升细粒度食物理解能力，需引入外部语义知识以增强模型区分能力。

Method: 提出LIM-F与LIM-Q两个模块，分别用于图像编码器和解码器阶段，通过大语言模型生成的食材标签，将语义信息注入视觉特征流中，实现无需预对齐的多模态融合。

Result: 在FoodSeg103数据集上，所提方法取得显著性能提升，最高达55.0 mIoU；LIM-F在多种架构中均表现出良好泛化能力，且训练内存开销可控（≤3.8 GB）。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效、可扩展的细粒度食物分割解决方案，通过融合大语言模型生成的语义信息，显著提升了复杂场景下的分割精度，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Food image segmentation plays a vital role in health-related applications such as nutrition tracking and personalized health monitoring. However, existing models often underperform on visually similar ingredients and rare food categories. To address this issue, we propose two plug-and-play multimodal modules that enhance the segmentation performance by leveraging ingredient labels inferred from food images using large language models (LLMs). The first module, called LIM-F (Language Injection Module for Features), is designed to pair with any image encoder that produces multi-layer outputs (e.g., Swin Transformer), while the second module, LIM-Q (Language Injection Module for Queries), targets Mask2Former-style Transformer-based decoders. Both modules enable training without the need for pre-aligning images with text by directly injecting semantic ingredient information into the visual analysis pipeline. On the FoodSeg103 benchmark, the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance. Specifically, integrating LIM-Q into the Mask2Former decoder with a Swin-L image encoder yields a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 55.0. LIM-F also demonstrates strong generalization and competitive performance, reaching an mIoU of 54.4 under the same model (Swin-L+Mask2Former). Furthermore, its applicability extends beyond Transformer-based decoders, as evidenced by an improvement from 47.7 to 49.8 mIoU when integrated into a CNN-based architecture. Notably, the improved segmentation accuracy is achieved with only a moderate (at most 3.8 GB) increase in the GPU memory consumption during training. Thus, the proposed approach offers a practical and scalable solution for fine-grained food understanding.

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Static Costs: Learning-Dynamics Aware Loss Functions for Long-Tailed Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25830)
*Varad Shinde,Nikhil Kumar Shrey,Magesh Rajasekaran,Md Saiful Islam Sajol,Harshil Bhargava,Subhajit Sidanta,Supratik Mukhopadhyay,Yimin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新型学习动态感知损失函数（LDAL），通过动态调整类别权重来解决长尾数据集中的类别不平衡问题。LDAL结合特征表示强度、预测熵和跨轮次预测变化，实现对模型学习过程的实时响应，无需额外计算开销，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重加权方法依赖静态类别频率，忽略了模型在训练过程中对不同类别学习进度的动态变化，导致对少数类别的性能不佳。为提升模型在长尾分布下的泛化能力，需要一种能随学习过程自适应调整权重的方法。

Method: 提出学习动态感知损失（LDAL），通过三个核心机制动态调整类别权重：(i) 基于特征表示强度（语义尺度）衡量类别的学习程度；(ii) 利用预测的香农熵评估类别的内在学习难度；(iii) 引入跨轮次正则项以监测预测变化，稳定训练并避免陷入局部最优。该方法作为纯目标函数，计算开销极低。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，LDAL显著优于当前最先进的重加权损失函数，在准确率与泛化能力之间实现了更优平衡，尤其在少数类别上表现大幅提升。

Conclusion: LDAL通过捕捉模型学习过程中的动态特性，有效缓解了长尾数据集带来的偏差问题，是一种高效且可推广的解决方案，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Deep learning models in computer vision face significant challenges when trained on long-tailed datasets, where a few majority classes dominate while many minority classes are severely underrepresented. Such imbalances frequently arise in real-world scenarios such as rare species recognition, manufacturing fault detection, and medical image understanding, leading to biased models that underperform on tail classes. Existing reweighting methods typically rely on static class frequencies to penalize the model, ignoring the dynamic nature of how effectively a network actually learns a class over time. We address this by introducing a novel Learning-Dynamics Aware Loss (LDAL) function that shifts the focus from static sample counts to dynamic learning progress. LDAL framework adjusts class weights continuously by leveraging: (i) the strength of learned feature representations (semantic scale), (ii) the intrinsic learning difficulty of each class, measured via the Shannon entropy of its predictions, and (iii) an inter-epoch regularizer term that tracks prediction shifts between consecutive epochs to stabilize training and avoid local minima. LDAL is purely a objective function which incurs negligible computational overhead while adapting to the feature learning of the model. Experimental results on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly surpasses state-of-the-art reweighting loss functions, providing an optimal trade-off between accuracy and generalizability. The source code is available at https://github.com/sdm2026/ldal

</details>


### [72] [Adversarial Deepfake Generation and an Investigation of Purification-Based Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25842)
*Junghyun Kim,Seunghyun Kim,Jiyoung Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 团队'Go To Germany'参与ImageCLEF 2026深伪检测与生成任务。在图像生成方面，采用FLUX.1-dev与PuLID实现保身份人脸合成，并结合多模型PGD对抗攻击，成功规避90%官方检测器和57.6%参赛检测器，生成得分0.4170；在图像检测方面，融合SigLIP+DINOv2（AI生成）与GenD-DINOv3（人脸篡改）的集成模型，基准深伪检测准确率达99.4%，但真实图像误报率高，最终检测得分0.6986。额外研究发现，经中值-3净化后的原始|Δlogit|信号在四类对抗样本中表现优异（AUROC 0.81–0.98），揭示了JPEG质量在Q70处存在显著性能断崖，挑战了单纯依赖主干网络保留的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升深伪图像生成的隐蔽性并增强检测系统的鲁棒性，同时探索对抗样本检测的新信号机制，以突破现有方法局限。

Method: 生成任务：使用FLUX.1-dev与PuLID进行身份保持的面部合成，结合针对12个检测器的多模型PGD对抗攻击；检测任务：采用基于SigLIP+DINOv2与GenD-DINOv3的极大概率集成策略；额外分析：对比六种共享CLIP ViT-L/14主干的检测器，评估三种检测信号在中值-3净化下的表现。

Result: 生成任务：对主办方检测器的规避率为90%，对参赛者检测器为57.6%，生成得分0.4170；检测任务：基准深伪识别准确率99.4%，但真实图像误报率高，最终检测得分0.6986；自研分析显示，|Δlogit|在中值-3净化下于四类对抗样本中表现出高区分能力（AUROC 0.81–0.98），且在JPEG质量降至Q70时性能骤降。

Conclusion: 所提生成方法可有效绕过主流检测系统，检测集成策略虽高效但需优化真实图像误报；更重要的是，发现净化后|Δlogit|信号具有强区分能力，揭示了对抗攻击与图像压缩质量间的非线性敏感关系，为未来检测方法设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of team "Go To Germany" in the ImageCLEF 2026 Deepfake Detection and Generation Task. For the image generation task, we employ FLUX.1-dev with PuLID for identity-preserving face synthesis, combined with a multi-model PGD adversarial attack targeting 12 detectors simultaneously (DiffJPEG-in-loop, MI/DI/EoT, adaptive weighting, two-stage warm-start). Our approach achieved 90% evasion against organizer detectors and 57.6% against participant detectors, with a final generation score of 0.4170. For the image detection task, we combine two complementary detectors - SigLIP+DINOv2 for AI-generated images and GenD-DINOv3 for face manipulations - in a max-probability ensemble, achieving 99.4% accuracy on baseline deepfakes but suffering from high false-positive rates on real images, resulting in a final detection score of 0.6986. Beyond the official submission, we conducted a self-initiated investigation of purification-based adversarial detection, comparing three families of detection signals across six detectors that share a CLIP ViT-L/14 backbone. We find that raw $|Δ\text{logit}|$ under median-3 purification, applied through the EFFORT detector, separates adversarial inputs from clean inputs with AUROC 0.81-0.98 across four adversarial source types - a finding that refutes the simple backbone-preservation hypothesis and exposes a sharp JPEG-quality cliff at Q70 where the signal collapses.

</details>


### [73] [Open-Ended CT Volume Segmentation with Weak Supervision from Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25860)
*Sanjay Subramanian,Junwei Yu,Zirui Wang,Rohil Malpani,Maggie Chung,Adam Yala,Dan Klein,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合体素级监督与可扩展的切片级监督的文本条件分割模型训练方法，用于CT扫描。通过从大量扫描-报告对中提取病灶描述及其对应的切片索引，利用弱监督信息微调通用2D图像分割模型SAM3，显著提升分割性能。在ReXGroundingCT数据集上，当仅有250个完全标注体积时，分割Dice分数相对提升达22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割依赖大量精细标注数据，但获取成本高；而临床报告中包含丰富的病灶位置信息，可作为低成本的弱监督信号，因此探索如何有效利用报告中的切片级描述来辅助训练分割模型。

Method: 从大规模扫描-报告对中提取病灶描述及对应切片索引，构建切片级弱监督信号；在此基础上，微调2D分割模型SAM3，同时使用强监督的分割损失和弱监督的切片分类损失进行联合训练。

Result: 在ReXGroundingCT数据集上，该方法显著提升了分割性能：当有1000个完全标注样本时，Dice分数相对提升8%；当仅有250个完全标注样本时，提升高达22%。

Conclusion: 结合体素级精细标注与来自报告的切片级弱监督，能够有效降低对全标注数据的依赖，并显著提升文本条件分割模型在CT扫描中的表现。

Abstract: We introduce a method for training a text-conditioned segmentation model for CT scans, which combines voxel-level supervision with coarse but scalable slice-level supervision from reports. We extract, from a large database of scan-report pairs, descriptions of findings with indices of slices where those findings occur. We then finetune a general-purpose 2D image segmentation model, SAM3, with standard segmentation losses from strongly labeled data and with a slice-level classification loss from the extracted weak supervision. Our results on the ReXGroundingCT dataset illustrate that this strategy improves the segmentation dice score: from an 8% relative gain when there are 1000 fully labeled volumes to 22% when there are 250 fully labeled volumes.

</details>


### [74] [TIGA: Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack against Black-box AIGC Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25894)
*Xia Du,Zhuosen Bao,Zheng Lin,Jizhe Zhou,Jiawei Lian,Chi-man Pun,Jun Luo,Wei Ni,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIGA是一种无需源图像且无需训练的生成攻击框架，通过在单个扩散采样轨迹中调整潜在扩散模型（DDIM）路径，使对抗性特征在生成过程中自然出现，而非事后添加。它利用多个白盒替代检测器的梯度生成可迁移的符号感知先验，并通过对称有限差分查询估计黑盒目标响应，结合衰减动量稳定方向，并按DDIM噪声调度注入，辅以频域重塑以抑制高频伪影。实验表明，TIGA在替代和未见的专业伪造检测器上均表现出强大的黑盒攻击性能、良好的迁移能力及对常见后处理操作的高鲁棒性，同时保持高感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反检测方法通常需扰动已生成图像或依赖检测器感知训练，易引入可见或统计伪影，且当扩散模型冻结、目标检测器仅可通过黑盒查询访问时适用性受限。因此亟需一种无需源图像、无需重训练、能有效规避黑盒检测器的新方法。

Method: TIGA首先聚合多个白盒替代检测器的梯度，构建可迁移的符号感知先验；随后采用对称有限差分查询进行各向异性方向搜索，估计黑盒目标响应；利用衰减动量稳定估计方向，并依据DDIM噪声调度将方向注入生成过程；最后通过频域重塑抑制高频伪影，实现高效且隐蔽的对抗生成。

Result: TIGA在多种替代和未见过的专用伪造检测器上均展现出强黑盒攻击效果，具备优异的迁移性与对常见后处理的鲁棒性，且无需源图像或重新训练扩散模型，同时保持高质量的视觉感知效果。

Conclusion: TIGA为扩散模型生成内容提供了一种高效、隐蔽且通用的反检测方案，突破了传统方法对训练或源图像的依赖，在实际应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models have achieved remarkable realism in facial image synthesis, posing growing challenges to artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) forensic detectors.Existing evasion methods typically perturb pre-generated images or require detector-aware training, which may introduce visible or statistical artifacts and limit applicability when the diffusion model must remain frozen and the target detector is accessible only through black-box queries. We propose Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack (TIGA), a source-image-free and training free framework that generates detector-evasive images within a single diffusion sampling trajectory. TIGA steers the latent Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) trajectory so that adversarial properties emerge during generation rather than being added afterward. TIGA first aggregates gradients from multiple white-box surrogate detectors to form a transferable, sign-aware prior, and then performs anisotropic directional search with symmetric finite-difference queries to estimate the black-box target response. The estimated directions are stabilized by decayed momentum and injected according to the DDIM noise schedule, with frequency-domain reshaping to suppress high frequency artifacts. Experiments on surrogate and unseen specialized forensic detectors show that TIGA achieves strong blackbox attack performance, transferability, and high robustness under common post-processing operations without source images or diffusion-model retraining, while preserving high perceptual quality.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluating VLMs for Autonomous Agent-Driven Geometry Clipping Detection in Video Game QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25921)
*Carlos Celemin,Benedict Wilkins,Adrián Barahona-Ríos,Saman Zadtootaghaj,Nabajeet Barman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在代理驱动的游戏质量保证（QA）流程中检测几何裁剪异常的表现。通过自定义探索代理收集视觉观测并结合自动标注的帧级裁剪标签，实现了无需人工标注的零样本异常检测评估。在六种主流VLM（Gemini、GPT、Qwen、Gemma、Llama、Ministral）上测试四种提示变体，结果表明所有模型均存在对视觉模糊帧（如近接触、部分遮挡）的高误报率。Gemini-3.1-Flash表现最佳，具有最高准确率和对提示变化的鲁棒性；而开源模型则表现出显著的精确率-召回率波动。结论指出，当前VLM更适合作为多阶段QA流程中的高召回候选过滤器，而非独立的缺陷检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏质量保证流程依赖大量人工标注，成本高且效率低。利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）实现自动化异常检测，可减少对人工标注的依赖，提升效率。尤其在几何裁剪这类视觉敏感问题上，探索VLM在零样本条件下识别异常的能力，具有重要实践意义。

Method: 构建一个代理驱动的游戏质量保证环境：使用自定义探索代理在游戏关卡中导航，采集视觉帧；采用自动标注系统生成帧级几何裁剪标签。在此基础上，对六种近期主流VLMs（Gemini、GPT、Qwen、Gemma、Llama、Ministral）进行零样本提示测试，分析其在四种不同提示设计下的表现差异。

Result: 所有VLM均能捕捉与几何裁剪相关的视觉线索，但在视觉模糊场景（如近接触、部分遮挡）中普遍存在大量误报。Gemini-3.1-Flash在整体准确率和提示鲁棒性方面表现最优；开源模型则随提示设计变化呈现剧烈的精确率-召回率波动。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型更适合作为多阶段质量保证流程中的高召回候选筛选工具，而非独立的缺陷检测系统。其在复杂视觉场景下的误报问题仍需通过后处理或集成其他检测模块来缓解。

Abstract: In this work, we study the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for anomaly detection in an agent-driven game Quality Assurance (QA) pipeline focusing on geometry clipping. In this evaluation, a custom exploration agent navigates a game level to collect visual observations, while the automatic annotation pipeline provides frame-level clipping labels. This setup allows us to evaluate recent VLMs on a controlled anomaly detection task without manual annotation. We benchmark six recent VLMs (Gemini, GPT, Qwen, Gemma, Llama, and Ministral) under a zero-shot prompting setting and analyse their sensitivity to four prompt variants.
  Our results show that while the VLMs can capture visual cues associated with geometry clipping, they all produce substantial false positives on visually ambiguous frames such as near-contact geometry and partial occlusions. Gemini-3.1-Flash achieves the best overall accuracy and is the most robust to prompt variation, while open-source models exhibit large precision--recall swings depending on the prompt design. These findings suggest that current VLMs are best suited as high-recall candidate filters within multi-stage QA pipelines rather than as standalone bug detectors.

</details>


### [76] [Face De-Identification: A Domain-Centric Survey from Capture to Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25926)
*Hui Wei,Hao Yu,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地综述了从物理、传感器到数字域的完整数据采集流程中的面部去标识（Face De-ID）技术，涵盖隐私保护方法在不同阶段的应用与挑战，并分析现有评估协议，强调建立标准化基准的重要性。最后提出关键开放问题和未来研究方向，附带项目主页提供文献、数据集与开源代码支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据隐私和负责任AI的重视，面部去标识成为重要研究领域。传统方法多在数字域进行后处理，但新兴趋势将隐私机制融入传感器或物理层面，需统一视角梳理全链条技术进展与挑战。

Method: 采用领域中心视角，对物理、传感器、数字三个阶段的面部去标识方法进行系统分析；归纳现有评估方法，对比实践差异；识别核心问题并展望未来方向。

Result: 构建了跨域统一的面部去标识研究框架，揭示各阶段技术特点与瓶颈，推动建立标准化评估体系，并为后续研究提供清晰指引。

Conclusion: 面部去标识正从单一后处理向端到端隐私设计演进，亟需跨领域协作与标准化建设。未来研究应聚焦于多域协同、真实场景适应性及可衡量的隐私-效用平衡。

Abstract: Face de-identification (De-ID) aims to remove or conceal personally identifiable facial features in images or videos to prevent identity recognition while preserving utility for downstream tasks. With the rising emphasis on data privacy and responsible AI, face De-ID has emerged as an active research area spanning computer vision and privacy-preserving communities. Early approaches, and many contemporary ones, operate in the digital domain by modifying pixel-level or appearance-level features through post-capture processing. Recent advances extend face De-ID beyond post-processing by integrating privacy mechanisms directly into sensors during image acquisition, bridging sensing systems and downstream vision algorithms. In parallel, physical-domain methods explore wearable accessories and materials that conceal identity information in real-world environments prior to capture. In this survey, we present the first unified overview that spans the full data acquisition pipeline, encompassing the physical, sensor, and digital domains. Through this domain-centric lens, we systematically analyze current methodologies, technical progress, and the distinct challenges inherent to each stage. We then review and organize existing evaluation protocols, examining current practices and highlighting the critical need for standardized, comprehensive benchmarks. Finally, we identify key open problems and outline emerging research directions to guide future work in this rapidly evolving field. To support ongoing research, we maintain a project page that organizes relevant literature with collected datasets and open source code: https://github.com/CV-AC/Awesome-FaceDe-ID.

</details>


### [77] [MODUS: Decoder-Only Any-to-Any Modeling of Diverse Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25948)
*Mingqiao Ye,Zhaochong An,Zhitong Gao,Xian Liu,François Fleuret,Chuan Li,Amir Zadeh,Serge Belongie,Afshin Dehghan,Jesse Allardice,David Mizrahi,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Amir Zamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于解码器的任意模态到任意模态的多模态建模方法（Modus），通过统一架构实现所有模态对称输入输出，无需特定模态头或损失函数，支持链式生成和跨模态自验证，性能优于现有单模型方法，并在多个基准上表现与专用模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意模态到任意模态模型通常从头训练，难以利用强大的预训练解码器模型作为先验，且设计复杂，限制了泛化能力与应用灵活性。

Method: 采用解码器仅架构，将所有模态视为对称输入与输出，统一建模流程，避免模态特定组件，实现任意模态组合的自由生成与验证。

Result: Modus在多种基准测试中表现出色，具备良好的零样本性能，可替代多个专用模型或任务管道，支持复杂应用场景如链式生成与自验证。

Conclusion: Modus展示了基于解码器的统一多模态建模在灵活性、性能和可扩展性上的优势，为多模态学习提供了一个强大而通用的新范式。

Abstract: Any-to-any models predict any modality from any combination of others within a single network, a formulation used in multimodal vision and vision-language models, and increasingly in scientific domains such as ecology and astronomy. Existing any-to-any models are typically trained from scratch using encoder-decoder or diffusion architectures, impacting their performance and preventing them from using strong pre-trained decoder-only models as a prior. In this work, we investigate decoder-only any-to-any multimodal modeling, which treats all modalities symmetrically and supports arbitrary modalities as inputs and outputs without modality-specific heads, losses, or task pipelines. Because every modality is both an input and an output of the same model, the resulting model, named Modus, can support a range of applications, such as chained generation through intermediate modalities or cross-modal self-verification by scoring the model's own outputs with another generated modality. Modus demonstrates strong out-of-the-box performance and is competitive with specialist and multitask baselines using a single model across various benchmarks. All materials are open-sourced at https://modus-multimodal.epfl.ch/.

</details>


### [78] [Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25961)
*Podakanti Satyajith Chary,Barath Parthiban,Pranesh Velmurugan,Adeeba Khan,Nagarajan Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM-AH 是一种用于识别健康行为改变中矛盾与犹豫（A/H）的多模态框架，通过分析视觉、音频和文本模态在时间上的冲突，利用轻量级流模型检测跨模态不一致、预测犹豫信号并发现行为原型。该方法结合密集窗口标注与元数据条件，通过知识引导的大语言模型进行推理，并在验证性能提升时进行后期融合，在525个视频的公开测试集上达到0.6133的宏平均F1，显著优于零样本基线（0.2827），且推理增益可泛化至更大测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别健康行为改变前的矛盾与犹豫状态具有重要临床意义，但因多模态信号间存在冲突且个体差异大，导致视频层面的识别困难，亟需一种能捕捉动态多模态冲突的智能分析框架。

Method: 采用冻结的视觉、音频和文本编码器对短时间窗进行对齐，输入轻量级流模型以评分跨模态不一致性，预测下一窗口的犹豫惊喜信号，挖掘行为原型，并结合参与者元数据进行条件化；通过密集窗口级标注作为辅助监督目标，校准决策阈值以优化宏平均F1；最后引入知识引导的大语言模型基于专家线索分类体系进行结构化推理，仅在验证性能提升时进行晚期融合。

Result: 在525个视频的公开测试集上，PRISM-AH取得0.6133的宏平均F1，远超零样本基线（0.2827）；推理模块带来的性能增益在更大测试集上依然有效，证明其泛化能力。

Conclusion: PRISM-AH成功实现了对多模态矛盾与犹豫状态的精准识别，通过融合时间动态建模、跨模态冲突分析与知识引导推理，为健康行为干预提供了可解释、可扩展的技术支持。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are conflicting affective states that precede the delay or abandonment of health behaviour change. Recognition of A/H at the video level is difficult, since the signal arises from disagreement across and within facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily modalities, and manifests differently across individuals. The proposed PRISM-AH (Predictive Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition), is a framework that treats A/H as a multimodal conflict that unfolds over time. Frozen vision, audio, and text encoders are aligned into short time windows and passed to a lightweight streaming model that scores cross-modal dissonance, predicts each next window to expose a hesitation surprise signal, discovers behaviour prototypes, and is conditioned on participant metadata. Dense window-level annotations supervise the model as an auxiliary objective, and the decision threshold is calibrated for macro F1. A knowledge-guided large language model then reasons over structured evidence using the expert cue taxonomy of the dataset, and its verdict is fused late only when validation performance improves. On the labelled public test partition of 525 videos, PRISM-AH attains a macro F1 of 0.6133, compared to the reported zero-shot baseline of 0.2827. The reasoning gain is validated to transfer from validation to the larger test partition.

</details>


### [79] [LaP-Forensics: Latent-Pixel Consistency Guided Multimodal Reasoning for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25962)
*Can Wang,Yuhao Wang,Yushe Cao,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaP-Forensics提出了一种多模态框架，通过结合RGB语义与基于重建的取证证据来增强图像伪造检测。利用冻结的Stable Diffusion DDIM反演-重建模型生成固定参考，并通过残差图衡量局部一致性。独立投影器编码RGB图像和残差图，再由结构化Where-What-Why模型预测文本分析和伪影掩码。采用监督微调结合组相对策略优化（GRPO），奖励函数融合掩码重叠、输出结构和证据参考项，鼓励模型引用一致性图但不验证自由文本的真实性。额外图像级头融合RGB与DDIM残差特征。实验显示在跨生成器检测和合成伪影定位上表现优异。控制性分析支持残差流的有效性，但自由文本的忠实性与可靠性在后处理下仍存局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型产生的图像视觉瑕疵较少，使得仅依赖表面外观的检测器和解释方法失效。需要一种能捕捉深层生成痕迹的多模态取证方法，以应对高保真生成内容带来的检测挑战。

Method: 引入冻结的Stable Diffusion DDIM反演-重建模型作为固定参考，生成残差图以度量局部一致性；使用独立投影器编码RGB图像与残差图；采用结构化Where-What-Why模型进行文本分析与伪影掩码预测；通过监督微调与组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练，奖励函数包含掩码重叠、输出结构及证据参考项；附加图像级分类头融合多源特征。

Result: 在UniversalFakeDetect数据集上实现跨生成器伪造检测性能提升，在SynthScars基准上达到竞争性伪影定位效果；控制实验验证了残差流的有效性；自由文本的忠实性与可靠性在后处理下仍受限。

Conclusion: LaP-Forensics通过融合重建残差与多模态推理，有效提升了对高保真生成图像的伪造检测能力，尤其在跨生成器场景和伪影定位方面表现突出。然而，其生成文本的可信度仍受后处理影响，需进一步研究以增强解释的可靠性。

Abstract: Recent generative models can produce images with few obvious visual artifacts, weakening detectors and explanations that rely only on surface appearance. We present LaP-Forensics, a multimodal framework that augments RGB semantics with reconstruction-based forensic evidence. A frozen Stable Diffusion DDIM inversion-reconstruction model provides a fixed reconstruction reference, and its residual map measures local compatibility with that reference. Independent projectors encode the RGB image and residual map before a structured Where-What-Why model predicts a textual analysis and an artifact mask.Supervised fine-tuning is followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), whose reward combines mask overlap with output-structure and evidence-reference terms. These text-side terms encourage the model to refer to the consistency map but do not constitute a verifier of free-form textual truth. A separate image-level head fuses RGB and DDIM-residual class features. Experiments show cross-generator detection on UniversalFakeDetect and competitive artifact localization on the official SynthScars benchmark. Controlled cue-construction, inversion-horizon, component, reward-term, and counterfactual analyses support the utility of the residual stream under the evaluated settings, while free-form textual faithfulness and reliability under post-processing remain open limitations.

</details>


### [80] [Quasi-SVD: Learning a Lie-constrained matrix factorisation for real-time imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25967)
*Christopher Hahne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Quasi-SVD is a fast, parallelizable, differentiable matrix factorization method for GPUs that sacrifices exact orthogonality for speed and real-time feasibility, achieving high reconstruction quality (SSIM 0.89–0.94) and >25 FPS performance across diverse medical imaging tasks, making it viable for real-time clinical use.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVD algorithms are inherently sequential, limiting real-time GPU throughput and hindering online deployment in clinical imaging pipelines despite their importance in computational imaging.

Method: Introduces Quasi-SVD, a differentiable, fully parallelized matrix factorization framework for GPUs that enforces orthogonality on only one Lie-parameterized factor while using soft constraints for the rest, enabling efficient parallel decomposition without iterative singular-vector optimization.

Result: Achieves SSIM of 0.89–0.94, accelerates computation by 3–20x compared to cuSOLVER and randomized SVD, enables throughput above 25 FPS, and demonstrates robust performance across two medical imaging tasks: spatio-temporal background subtraction in ultrasound localization microscopy and Mueller matrix polarimetry for neurosurgical tissue characterization.

Conclusion: By prioritizing reconstruction fidelity over exact spectral recovery, Quasi-SVD enables practical, real-time structured matrix factorization suitable for live image-guided clinical workflows, overcoming limitations of classical solvers.

Abstract: Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) underlies matrix factorisation tasks across computational imaging, with medical applications increasingly demanding real-time processing. Yet SVD algorithms are inherently sequential, constraining real-time GPU throughput and limit online deployment in clinical pipelines. This study introduces Quasi-SVD, a differentiable, fully parallelized matrix factorization framework for GPUs. Rather than enforcing orthogonality on both factors, it guarantees exact orthogonality for a single Lie-parameterized factor while recovering the remaining components through soft constraints, enabling efficient parallel decomposition without iterative singular-vector optimization. This asymmetric design, provably sufficient for valid factorisation, achieves reconstruction fidelity of SSIM = 0.89-0.94 and accelerates computation by 3-20x relative to cuSOLVER and randomised SVD, enabling throughput above 25 FPS. Performance is evaluated on two medical imaging tasks spanning complementary computational regimes: (1) spatio-temporal background subtraction for ultrasound localisation microscopy, requiring high-dimensional matrix separation, and (2) Mueller matrix polarimetry for neurosurgical tissue characterisation, requiring massive batch processing of small matrices. Across both regimes and multiple imaging instruments, the proposed framework demonstrates robust domain transfer and throughput exceeding 25 FPS at clinical matrix scales, a rate sufficient for live image-guided workflows that classical solvers cannot currently support in these settings. By prioritising downstream reconstruction fidelity over exact spectral recovery, Quasi-SVD makes structured matrix factorisation practical for real-time imaging.

</details>


### [81] [Schrödinger's Cat: Probabilistic Representation and Prediction of Potential Scene Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25984)
*Timy Phan,Jannik Wiese,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GARFIELD 是一种概率模型，用于预测场景未来可能的运动分布。它通过学习结构化的时空潜在表示，实现对多个可能轨迹的联合采样和高效密度估计，显著提升运动规划性能并加快采样与密度计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在视频预测中或侧重外观生成，或仅采样少量轨迹，缺乏对运动分布的显式建模。为解决这一问题，需要一种能够有效捕捉多可能性未来、支持高效推理和不确定性定位的模型。

Method: 提出 GARFIELD 模型，利用结构化时空潜在表示来建模给定图像及稀疏约束下的未来运动分布，并通过确定性密度解码器直接访问运动分布，支持联合采样与逐步优化。

Result: 实验表明，GARFIELD 在运动规划性能上媲美大型视频生成模型，轨迹采样速度提升 97 倍；运动密度估计速度比蒙特卡洛采样快两个数量级，支持交互式探索与不确定性感知规划。

Conclusion: GARFIELD 实现了对未来运动分布的高效、精确建模，具备高效率与可解释性，在运动预测与规划任务中展现出强大潜力。

Abstract: Predicting how a scene may evolve from partial observations requires reasoning about multiple possible futures rather than committing to a single trajectory. Existing approaches either generate appearance-dominated video predictions or sample a small number of trajectories without explicitly modeling the distribution of possible motion. We introduce Goal-Aware Representations of Future kInEmatic Latent Distributions (GARFIELD), a probabilistic model of scene kinematics that learns a structured spatio-temporal latent representation of the distribution over possible futures given an image and optional spatio-temporally sparse constraints. The same latent representation enables both joint sampling of all trajectories and direct access to the underlying motion distribution through an efficient deterministic density decoder. As a result, uncertainty about future motion can be localized to specific scene elements and timesteps and progressively refined through additional constraints. Experiments demonstrate strong motion planning performance competitive with large video generation models while sampling trajectories $97\times$ faster. Our method further estimates motion densities two orders of magnitude faster than Monte-Carlo sampling from motion generation models, enabling interactive exploration and uncertainty-aware planning.

</details>


### [82] [On the Use of Synthetic Data for Threshold Calibration in Face Recognition: Performance and Security Implications for Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25990)
*Arto Apila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在欧洲边境控制的文档到活体验证场景中，使用合成人脸数据进行阈值校准的有效性。尽管合成数据在受控环境下能近似真实数据的校准行为，但在非受限条件下因得分分布尾部不匹配而无法可靠泛化，导致识别性能下降和易受变形攻击。研究强调，高安全性部署中的阈值选择仍需依赖代表性真实世界数据进行验证与调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前欧盟入境/出境系统（EES）要求人脸识别系统在极低误匹配率（FMR）下运行，但成员国层面缺乏具体的阈值校准指导。由于法律、物流和隐私限制，获取真实世界数据用于校准困难，因此需要探索替代方案如合成数据的可行性。

Method: 通过分析合成数据与真实数据在真实样本和伪造样本得分分布上的对齐情况，评估不同域间阈值校准的可迁移性，重点关注低FMR操作点的表现。

Result: 合成数据在受控条件下可近似校准行为，但在非受限条件下因得分分布尾部差异导致性能显著下降，并增加对基于变形攻击的脆弱性；且校准结果高度依赖具体数据集，即使在合成数据之间也存在差异。

Conclusion: 合成数据虽有助于系统开发和初步校准，但在高安全性的实际部署中，可靠的阈值选择仍需基于代表性的真实世界数据进行验证与调整。

Abstract: The recently deployed Entry/Exit System (EES) introduces large-scale biometric verification into European border control, requiring face recognition systems to operate at extremely low false match rates (FMR). While regulatory frameworks define performance targets at the EES Central System level, they do not specify how verification thresholds should be calibrated in practice at the Member State level. In operational settings, obtaining representative real-world data for calibration is often constrained by legal, logistical, and privacy limitations.
  In this work, we investigate the use of synthetic face data for threshold calibration in document-to-live verification scenarios relevant to border control systems. We analyze the alignment of genuine and impostor score distributions between synthetic and real datasets and evaluate the transferability of calibrated thresholds across domains, with a focus on low-FMR operating points.
  Our results show that synthetic data can approximate calibration behavior in controlled settings, but fails to reliably generalize to unconstrained conditions due to mismatches in score distribution tails. These discrepancies lead to significant degradation in recognition performance and increased vulnerability to morph-based attacks. We further demonstrate that calibration outcomes are highly dataset-dependent, even across synthetic datasets.
  Overall, our findings highlight that while synthetic data is useful for system development and preliminary calibration, our results indicate that reliable threshold selection in high-security deployments typically requires validation and adjustment using representative real-world data.

</details>


### [83] [Beyond Zooming: Learning Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25993)
*Fengxiang Wang,Jiangnan Huang,Mingshuo Chen,Yueying Li,Yang Shi,Junwei Luo,Haoyu Wang,Yansheng Li,Jing Zhang,Haiyan Zhao,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对超高清遥感影像中任务相关证据稀疏、局部且分散的问题，提出GeoMTVR数据集和GeoLens多工具视觉推理模型。通过引入多样化工具调用与推理轨迹，结合监督微调与基于工具注意力的强化学习，显著提升模型在复杂地理空间任务中的表现，优于单一缩放工具或直接推理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清遥感影像虽提供精细地表观测信息，但其大尺度、局部稀疏的证据分布对多模态大语言模型构成挑战；现有单工具缩放方法在复杂任务中效果有限，需更强大的多工具协同推理能力。

Method: 构建大规模地理空间多工具视觉推理数据集GeoMTVR，包含13,000个超高清遥感问答样本及交错的推理轨迹、多样工具调用与视觉反馈；提出一种聚焦工具注意力的强化学习算法，优化工具调用时机、选择、位置及输出解释；结合监督微调与强化学习训练GeoLens模型。

Result: GeoLens在多项任务上超越直接推理与单工具缩放基线，实现更高准确率、更强证据定位能力与更高效的工具使用路径，在复杂地理分析任务中表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过引入多工具协同推理机制与专用训练策略，GeoLens有效解决了超高清遥感图像中稀疏证据的全局推理难题，为未来高精度地理空间智能分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote-sensing (RS) imagery provides fine-grained Earth-observation evidence over city-scale scenes, but poses a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs): task-relevant evidence is often sparse, local, and spatially dispersed across extremely large visual contexts. A natural solution is to equip MLLMs with zoom-in tools for active local inspection. However, through a pilot study on XLRS-Bench, we find that zoom-in is only partially effective: it resolves easy and medium-level tasks with locally recoverable evidence, but saturates on hard cases requiring global search, multi-region comparison, path planning, or dispersed-evidence reasoning. Motivated by this finding, we move beyond single-tool zoom-in and introduce GeoMTVR, a large-scale Geospatial Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning dataset built from wide-area satellite imagery. GeoMTVR contains 13K UHR VQA samples with interleaved reasoning trajectories, diverse visual tool calls, and returned visual observations, enabling models to learn question decomposition, tool selection, regional inspection, object-level grounding, auxiliary visual reasoning, and cross-tool evidence integration. Beyond supervised fine-tuning, we propose a tool-attention-focused reinforcement learning algorithm that concentrates optimization on critical tool-use decisions, including when to invoke tools, which tool to select, where to apply it, and how to interpret tool outputs. By combining SFT on GeoMTVR with our RL algorithm, we develop GeoLens, a multi-tool visual reasoning MLLM for UHR RS. Experiments show that GeoLens consistently outperforms direct reasoning and single-tool zoom-in baselines, achieving stronger accuracy, better evidence grounding, and more efficient tool-use trajectories.

</details>


### [84] [Parallel Decoding Distillation for Fast Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26004)
*Neta Shaul,Chao Liu,Arash Vahdat,Julius Berner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PDD提出一种简化且可扩展的基于轨迹的蒸馏方法，用于加速扩散模型和流匹配模型的视频生成。通过在每次网络评估中预测多个去噪步骤，实现4-8次函数求值（NFE）下的SOTA性能，同时显著提升视频多样性，兼容任意预训练模型并支持可变NFE采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA加速方法依赖于变分分数蒸馏（VSD）和对抗性损失，但这些损失难以优化且易导致模式崩溃，造成视频多样性下降和运动缺失。因此需要一种更稳定、高效的蒸馏方法来加速生成过程并保持高质量与多样性。

Method: 提出Parallel Decoding Distillation（PDD），采用轨迹驱动的蒸馏策略，不直接回归导数，而是学习均值速度的表示，避免使用JVP或有限差分近似；通过一次网络评估预测多个去噪步骤，支持灵活的NFE设置。

Result: 在LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio、Wan 14B Text-to-Video和Qwen-Image Text-to-Image数据集上，PDD仅用4-8 NFE即达到SOTA性能，并显著提升了生成视频的多样性。

Conclusion: PDD是一种高效、稳定且可扩展的蒸馏方法，能够显著加速视频生成，同时保持高质量与高多样性，适用于多种预训练模型和不同的采样需求。

Abstract: Generation in video diffusion or flow models is computationally expensive due to the slow and iterative sampling process. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) acceleration methods heavily rely on variational score distillation (VSD) and adversarial losses to distill diffusion models into few-step generators. Albeit achieving high-quality video generation, these training losses are notoriously hard to optimize and suffer from mode collapse, leading to loss of video diversity and lack of motion. In this paper, we introduce Parallel Decoding Distillation (PDD), a simplified and scalable trajectory-based distillation method for fast inference of diffusion and flow matching models. Our architecture and training procedure are compatible with any pre-trained model and support sampling with a varying number of function evaluations (NFE). PDD accelerates generation by predicting multiple denoising steps per network evaluation. Conceptually, it learns a representation of the mean velocity without regressing its derivative using JVPs or finite-difference approximations. Our method achieves SOTA performance with 4-8 NFE on LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio, Wan 14B Text-to-Video, and Qwen-Image Text-to-Image. Moreover, PDD presents a significant improvement in generated video diversity.

</details>


### [85] [Pictura: Perspective-View Self-Play at Scale for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26005)
*Yuan Yin,Elias Ramzi,Marc Lafon,Valentin Charraut,Victor Bares,Yihong Xu,Éloi Zablocki,Alexandre Boulch,Thibault Buhet,Andrei Bursuc,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Pictura，一个基于GPU加速的多智能体驾驶模拟器，能够实时渲染每个代理的前视视角图像，从而在训练中直接使用视角图像而非特权向量观测，实现从视角图像端到端训练驾驶策略。通过在Pictura上进行大规模自对弈训练（500亿次代理步），Alberti模型在性能上接近使用特权观测的基准模型，并在零样本迁移至Waymo Open Motion Dataset时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自对弈训练依赖于特权向量观测（如精确位置、速度），但这些观测与实际部署中摄像头视角的局部观测存在表示差距，导致知识蒸馏后的学生模型无法合理解释自身决策。为解决这一问题，需在训练阶段就使用真实的视角图像，消除表示鸿沟。

Method: 提出Pictura模拟器，支持每一步渲染各智能体的前视视角图像；采用标准PPO算法在纯图像输入下进行大规模自对弈训练，构建直接从视角图像学习的驾驶策略（Alberti）。

Result: Alberti在50B代理步训练后达到与特权观测模型相当的驾驶性能，并在零样本迁移至重新渲染的Waymo数据集布局时超越特权观测模型。该方法在单个H100 GPU上实现高达50万代理步/秒的训练效率。

Conclusion: 通过引入视角自对弈训练框架和Pictura模拟器，实现了无需特权观测的端到端视觉驱动策略训练，显著缩小了仿真与真实部署之间的表示差距，为大规模自动驾驶策略训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Self-play in simulation produces robust driving policies at scale. Demonstrations of such behavior have been made using privileged vectorized observations such as exact poses and velocities, even for occluded agents. This assumes that perception is solved and introduces a representation gap with the partial observation of a deployed agent driving from the perspective view of egocentric cameras. A common fix, distilling the privileged policy into a camera-input student, leaves the student imitating decisions its own view cannot justify. Instead, we establish perspective-view self-play as a practical training regime. We introduce Pictura, a GPU-accelerated multi-agent driving simulator that renders each agent's egocentric view at every step, mitigating the representation gap at its source. Pictura sustains up to 500K agent-steps/s (2M images/s) on a single H100. Using Pictura, we train Alberti by self-play with plain PPO. It is the first large-scale driving self-play policy trained directly from perspective images, without privileged observations. Training spans 50B agent steps for ~35M km of driving. It approaches the driving performance of its privileged vectorized counterpart, and transfers zero-shot to Waymo Open Motion Dataset layouts re-rendered in Pictura, where it outperforms privileged vectorized agents. Project page: https://valeoai.github.io/Pictura/

</details>


### [86] [Wonder: Video World Model Done Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26037)
*Jiacong Xu,Hanwen Jiang,Zhixin Shu,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Vishal M. Patel,Yiqun Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wonder is a general-purpose video world model enabling real-time, camera-controllable exploration of synthesized environments. It uses a novel camera conditioning with dense coordinate fields for spatially aligned motion cues, an efficient sparse attention-based memory mechanism for fast retrieval, and improved distillation techniques to maintain control fidelity, diversity, and long-term coherence. The system generates diverse, high-fidelity videos at 16 FPS with consistent geometry and dynamics over extended sequences.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To enable real-time, interactive, and long-horizon video generation where users can freely navigate and explore virtual worlds by controlling the camera, requiring robust memory, precise control interpretation, and coherent scene synthesis.

Method: Introduces camera conditioning via a dense coordinate field to interpret camera motion as visual evidence; employs a sparse attention-based memory mechanism for efficient context retrieval; enhances self-forcing distillation to preserve control signals, diversity, and long-term memory.

Result: Wonder synthesizes diverse, minute-scale videos at 16 FPS with coherent geometry, appearance, and dynamics across long rollouts. It supports both image-to-video and video-conditioned generation, enabling real-time re-shooting of dynamic scenes.

Conclusion: Wonder achieves a significant advance in interactive video generation by co-designing control, memory, and training strategies, enabling real-time, camera-controllable exploration of rich, coherent, and long-term consistent virtual worlds.

Abstract: We present Wonder, a general-purpose video world model for real-time, camera-controllable world exploration. Given an image or a conditional video, Wonder constructs a playable world where users can navigate interactively by moving the camera, discovering unseen regions, and revisiting previously observed areas in real time and over a long-term horizon. Achieving this capability requires a system-level co-design of control method, memory mechanism, and training strategy. We introduce a novel camera conditioning with a dense coordinate field whose renderings provide spatially aligned motion and orientation cues, allowing the model to interpret camera motion directly as visual evidence. To support fast and precise memory retrieval over a growing generation context, we propose an efficient sparse attention-based memory mechanism, enabling the model to selectively attend to a small set of relevant context tokens at inference time, regardless of actual context length. We further develop several techniques to rectify the self-forcing-style distillation pipeline, improving the student model's ability to respect control signals, as well as maintaining diverse generation modes and long-term memory from the teacher. Together, these components enable Wonder to synthesize diverse, minute-scale videos at 16 FPS while preserving coherent geometry, appearance, and dynamics across long rollouts. Beyond image-to-video generation, Wonder naturally supports video-conditioned generation, allowing existing dynamic scenes to be re-shot in real time.

</details>


### [87] [VetClaw: An Edge-Cloud Multimodal Agentic System for Veterinary Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26042)
*Syed Mhamudul Hasan,Anas AlSobeh,Hussein Zangoti,Abdur R. Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VetClaw 是一个用于早期兽医疾病筛查的边缘-云多模态智能体系统，利用摄像头作为边缘感知设备，将图像和可选的症状描述发送至服务器端的视觉语言模型进行零样本疾病分类。系统通过 OpenClaw 与 LangGraph 分离交互与工作流管理：前者在边缘设备上提供调度、工具访问、用户交互和通知服务，后者负责状态化筛查流程，包括输入验证、图像传输、模型调用、安全检查、条件路由、故障处理和结构化日志记录。该设计超越了静态图像分类，实现了视觉证据收集、外部模型调用、确定性安全规则应用及诊断支持警报生成。实验表明，仅依赖图像的 VLM 预测性能有限，而结合症状描述的多模态输入显著提升零样本分类效果。因此，VetClaw 将静态预测模型升级为协调、安全感知的智能系统，具备工具使用、工作流管理、故障处理和不确定案例上报能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统兽医疾病筛查依赖人工经验，存在误诊率高、早期发现难的问题。现有基于图像的自动诊断系统多为静态分类模型，缺乏对复杂临床场景的适应性，无法处理不确定性、错误输入或需要多步骤推理的情况。为实现更可靠、可扩展的早期筛查，亟需一种能够整合多源信息、动态决策并保障安全性的智能系统。

Method: VetClaw 采用边缘-云协同架构：边缘侧部署 OpenClaw 智能体，负责本地感知、用户交互与任务调度；云端由 LangGraph 管理状态化的筛查工作流，集成输入验证、图像上传、调用视觉语言模型（VLM）、执行安全规则、条件判断、异常处理与日志记录。系统支持多模态输入（图像+文本症状描述），利用零样本学习实现跨病种疾病分类，并通过规则引擎增强安全性与可解释性。

Result: 实验结果显示，仅使用图像的 VLM 分类准确率受限，而加入症状描述后的多模态输入显著提升分类性能。系统能够有效识别模糊或高风险病例，触发预警并引导人工介入。整体流程具备良好的容错性与可追溯性，相比传统静态模型更具实用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VetClaw 成功构建了一个面向早期兽医疾病筛查的多模态智能体系统，通过分离智能体交互与工作流管理，实现了从静态图像分类向动态、安全、可扩展的诊疗辅助系统的演进。该系统在多模态输入下表现优异，具备实际部署潜力，为智能兽医诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present VetClaw, an edge-cloud multimodal agentic system for early veterinary disease screening. VetClaw uses a camera module as an edge sensing device and sends captured images, together with optional symptom descriptions, to a server-hosted vision-language model for zero-shot disease classification. The system separates agent interaction from workflow orchestration: OpenClaw provides scheduling, tool access, user interaction, and notification services on the edge device, while LangGraph manages the stateful screening workflow, including input validation, image transmission, model invocation, safety checks, conditional routing, failure handling, and structured logging. This design moves beyond static image classification by enabling the system to collect visual evidence, invoke external models, apply deterministic safety rules, and generate diagnostic-support alerts. Results show that image-only VLM prediction remains limited, whereas symptom-guided and multimodal inputs improve zero-shot classification performance. Thus, VetClaw transforms a static prediction model into a coordinated, safety-aware system that can use tools, manage workflows, handle failures, and escalate uncertain cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [TimeCapsule: Generative Hallucination as a Method for Historical Sensemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24750)
*Hayk Grigorian,Hamed Yaghoobian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TimeCapsule is a 1.2B-parameter LLaMA-style model trained solely on Victorian-era texts (1800–1875), designed to serve as a temporally isolated generative archive. It achieves a 45.4% lower perplexity than GPT-2 on Victorian prose, demonstrating strong fit to historical language. Despite lacking modern knowledge, it generates plausible analogical explanations for contemporary concepts using period-appropriate metaphors. Human evaluation revealed that scholars misidentified ~40% of authentic Victorian texts as AI-generated, highlighting a crisis of authenticity. The study argues that temporal ignorance enables hallucinations to function as interpretive tools into 19th-century worldviews.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large Language Models are overexposed to contemporary data, making them unreliable for historical understanding. This paper aims to create a temporally isolated model that reflects the epistemology of the 19th century, enabling more authentic historical reasoning and interpretation.

Method: Training a 1.2B-parameter causal language model (TimeCapsule) exclusively on Victorian-era texts (1800–1875). Evaluation includes quantitative perplexity comparison with GPT-2 and larger models, computational sensemaking tests (analogical explanation generation), and qualitative hermeneutic probes involving humanities scholars.

Result: TimeCapsule reduces perplexity by 45.4% compared to GPT-2 on held-out Victorian texts. Larger contemporary models achieve lower raw perplexity but lack temporal isolation. TimeCapsule generates historically plausible analogies for modern concepts (e.g., 'computer' as a 'hypertrophied lung'). Humanities scholars misclassified ~40% of real Victorian excerpts as machine-generated, indicating a breakdown in perceived authenticity.

Conclusion: Structural ignorance of the future allows LLM hallucinations to become interpretive probes into past ontologies. TimeCapsule demonstrates that temporal isolation enhances epistemic fidelity and reveals new ways to engage with historical meaning-making.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are temporally overexposed: trained on vast contemporary corpora, they encode present-day concepts that make them unreliable narrators of the past. We present TimeCapsule, a 1.2B-parameter LLaMA-style causal model trained exclusively on Victorian texts (1800-1875) as an epistemologically isolated generative archive. Quantitative evaluation shows a 45.4% perplexity reduction over a GPT-2 baseline on held-out Victorian prose, while larger contemporary causal models achieve lower raw perplexity through broader pretraining but lack temporal isolation. TimeCapsule exhibits computational sensemaking, generating historically plausible analogical explanations for unfamiliar modern concepts (e.g., describing a computer as a "hypertrophied lung"). A qualitative hermeneutic probe with two humanities scholars revealed a crisis of authenticity, as both misclassified approximately 40% of genuine Victorian excerpts as machine-produced. We argue that structural ignorance of the future transforms hallucinations into interpretive probes of nineteenth-century ontologies.

</details>


### [89] [Measuring and Improving Behavioral Consistency in Large Language Models through Fact-Heuristic-Emotion State Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24765)
*Gi-Hun Lee,Joong Yull Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出认知核模型（CKM），一种通过提示层状态强制机制提升大语言模型决策一致性的方法。CKM要求模型在决策前将输入分为事实、启发式和情感三类信息，并维护结构化状态。实验在韩语决策场景下对26个来自四个供应商的LLM进行测试，共37,403次观察。结果显示：CKM显著降低重复输出的变异性（效应量g=1.09），使新模型的决策翻转率下降82%；其效果不仅源于格式约束，更依赖于事实/假设/评价内容的分离；在采样随机性增加时优势更大；约45%的改进归因于结构框架，55%来自内容分层。尽管不提升推理正确性，但证明行为一致性可测量且部分可优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在相同决策问题上存在运行间不一致，且可能因自身先前答案作为上下文而反转决策。研究旨在探索这种不稳定性是否可被测量并部分减少，而不修改模型权重。

Method: 提出认知核模型（CKM），通过提示层引入结构化状态追踪机制，强制模型在决策前将输入划分为事实、启发式和情感三类信息，并使用过渡函数更新状态。采用四组核心实验、四臂消融实验、五臂假限制消融实验及温度探测实验进行评估。

Result: (1) CKM降低重复输出变异性（Hedges' g=1.09）；(2) 状态持续性使新模型决策翻转率下降82%（g=1.52）；(3) 效果非仅由JSON格式引起（值重计算g=2.24）；(4) 固定状态下的内在随机性可忽略；(5) 采样随机性增强时优势更明显（温度0.7时g=2.87）；(6) 假限制实验表明45%收益来自结构框架，55%来自内容分层，且仅CKM同时提升一致性和减少翻转。

Conclusion: CKM虽未提升推理正确性，但证明行为一致性可测量、跨模型差异明显，且可通过强制模型分离事实、假设与评价信号来部分改善。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can give different answers to the same decision problem across runs, and reverse a decision when their own prior answer returns as context. We ask whether this instability can be measured and partially reduced without changing model weights.
  We test the Cognitive Kernel Model (CKM), a prompt-level state-enforcement layer. Before deciding, the model must separate its input into three epistemic roles: Fact (given or verifiable), Heuristic (inferred or assumed), and Emotion (evaluative or priority signal). CKM adds no capability; it forces the model to track what kind of information it uses before acting. Formally it maintains a structured state S_t = {F_t, H_t, E_t} updated by a transition function.
  We evaluate CKM on Korean-language decision scenarios (ambiguity, ethical conflict, resource allocation, error handling) across 26 LLMs from four vendors and 37,403 observations, via four core experiments, a 4-arm ablation, a 5-arm sham-restriction ablation, and a temperature probe. Findings:
  (1) CKM reduces repeated-output variability (random-effects Hedges' g=1.09, 95% CI [0.83, 1.35], 31 model pairs);
  (2) state persistence cuts the decision-flip rate by 82% in newer models (g=1.52);
  (3) the effect is not JSON formatting alone (value-only recomputation, g=2.24);
  (4) intrinsic randomness under fixed anchor states is negligible;
  (5) the advantage grows under sampling stochasticity (g=2.87 at temperature 0.7);
  (6) a sham ablation attributes about 45% of the gain to structural scaffolding and 55% to Fact/Heuristic/Emotion content, and CKM is the only arm that both raises consistency and reduces flipping.
  CKM does not improve reasoning correctness. The narrower result: behavioral consistency is measurable, varies across models, and is partially improvable by forcing models to separate facts, assumptions, and evaluative signals before deciding.

</details>


### [90] [Neuromorphic Diffusion Language Models: Addressing Compute and Memory Bottlenecks via Sparsity and Block Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24841)
*Dengyu Wu,Clement Ruah,Jiechen Chen,Bipin Rajendran,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的神经形态掩码扩散语言模型（N-MDLM），通过结合块扩散与基于脉冲的神经形态计算，显著提升了大语言模型在推理阶段的吞吐量和能效。该方法利用块并行生成多个令牌以减少参数访问次数，并通过脉冲诱导的稀疏性跳过不活跃通道，降低有效参数流量与计算开销。作者构建了一个受屋顶模型启发的令牌级分析框架，量化了稀疏性与扩散协同效应。实验表明，即使在计算密集型平台，N-MDLM也相比传统自回归模型实现了更高的能效与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型在推理时效率低下，因每个生成令牌都需要访问全部模型参数，导致操作强度低、能耗高。尽管掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）能在内存受限场景下提升效率，但在计算密集型平台上仍存在局限。为更高效地利用现代芯片中丰富的片上内存，亟需一种能同时提升吞吐量与能效的新架构。

Method: 提出神经形态掩码扩散语言模型（N-MDLM），融合块扩散机制与脉冲神经网络计算；块扩散实现多令牌并行生成，减少参数访问频率；脉冲稀疏性通过激活信号跳过不活跃通道，降低计算与通信开销；构建令牌级屋顶模型分析稀疏性与扩散的协同增益。

Result: 在翻译任务上的实验表明，得益于脉冲诱导的稀疏性，N-MDLM在计算密集型平台也能显著超越自回归模型，在能效和吞吐量方面均有大幅提升，证明其在多种硬件环境下均具备优越性。

Conclusion: N-MDLM通过整合块扩散与脉冲稀疏性，成功实现了推理阶段的高吞吐与低能耗，为下一代高效大模型推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) large language models (LLMs) are inherently inefficient at inference time because each generated token requires accessing the full set of model parameters, leading to low operational intensity and high energy consumption. Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) partially address this limitation for memory-bound settings by allowing multiple tokens to be generated per parameter access. In order to further enhance inference efficiency on modern platforms with extensive in-chip memory, this work proposes neuromorphic MDLMs (N-MDLMs), which integrate block diffusion with spike-based neuromorphic computation to jointly improve throughput and energy efficiency. While block diffusion increases token throughput by producing multiple tokens per parameter access, spike-induced sparsity reduces effective parameter traffic and computations by skipping inactive channels. To analyze the synergistic effect of sparsity and diffusion, we develop a token-level roofline-inspired model that captures the combined impact of block-parallel generation and spike sparsity on decoding efficiency. Experimental results on translation tasks show that, thanks to spike-induced sparsity, N-MDLMs achieve substantial improvements in energy efficiency and throughput even in compute-bound platforms for which MDLMs would fail to improve over AR-LLMs.

</details>


### [91] [Research Report on Noise-Shaped One-Bit Coefficients in Discrete Polynomial Fourier Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24868)
*Shengquan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了归一化离散多项式傅里叶扩展中的噪声整形一位系数。针对一阶Sigma-Delta量化，误差表示为 $e_k=u_k-q_k=Δv_k$，其中状态均匀有界；通过离散求和分部法，得到复权重的变差估计，并在紧致参数集上获得 $O(N^{-1})$ 的逼近率。对于抛物相位 $φ_{x,t}(ξ)=xξ+tξ^2$，该界以 $J(x,t)=\int_0^1 |x+2tξ|dξ$ 表示，且在允许输入类上证明其为最优。推导出高阶有限记录恒等式并保留所有端点迹；在端点兼容或显式边界修正后，$r$ 阶噪声整形误差 $e=Δ^r v$ 可实现光滑权重下的 $O(N^{-r})$ 收敛，以及 $C^{r-1,α}$ 权重下的 $O(N^{-(r-1+α)})$ 收敛。建立了精确的 $L^2$ 正交恒等式、四阶矩公式、局部核估计及振荡转移界。还拓展至多项式相位、多维参数族、增长观测区域和相关状态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究噪声整形一位系数在离散傅里叶扩展中的逼近性能，旨在提高量化信号处理中对复杂相位函数的逼近精度，尤其关注在有限记录条件下如何通过高阶噪声整形实现更快的收敛速率。

Method: 采用离散求和分部法分析一阶与高阶噪声整形误差结构，结合端点迹保留的有限记录恒等式，利用正交性、矩公式与核估计工具，建立变差与逼近误差的统一框架，并通过边界修正与相位特性分析推广至更广模型。

Result: 在一阶情况下，获得 $O(N^{-1})$ 逼近率，且对抛物相位情形为最优；高阶噪声整形下，实现 $O(N^{-r})$（光滑权重）与 $O(N^{-(r-1+α)})$（Hölder连续权重）的收敛速度；建立了精确的正交恒等式、四阶矩公式、局部核估计与振荡转移界，模型可推广至多维、增长区域与相关状态情形。

Conclusion: 本研究系统建立了基于噪声整形的一位系数在多项式相位傅里叶扩展中的逼近理论，揭示了高阶噪声整形对逼近速率的显著提升作用，并为实际信号处理中低精度量化提供了理论支持。

Abstract: This report studies noise-shaped one-bit coefficients in normalized discrete polynomial Fourier extension. For first-order Sigma-Delta quantization, the error is written as $e_k=u_k-q_k=Δv_k$ with a uniformly bounded state. Discrete summation by parts then yields variation estimates for complex weights and an $O(N^{-1})$ approximation rate on compact parameter sets. For the parabolic phase $φ_{x,t}(ξ)=xξ+tξ^2$, the bound is expressed through $J(x,t)=\int_0^1 |x+2tξ|dξ$, and the uniform $N^{-1}$ rate is shown to be sharp over the admissible input class. Higher-order finite-record identities are derived with all endpoint traces retained. Under endpoint compatibility, or after explicit boundary correction, an $r$th-order noise-shaped error $e=Δ^r v$ gives $O(N^{-r})$ decay for sufficiently smooth weights and $O(N^{-(r-1+α)})$ decay for $C^{r-1,α}$ weights. Exact $L^2$ orthogonality identities, fourth-moment formulas, local kernel estimates, and oscillatory transfer bounds are also established. Extensions to polynomial phases, multidimensional parameter families, growing observation regions, and correlated state models are included.

</details>


### [92] [CogArena: A Multimethod Evaluation of Cognitive Ability Structure in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24999)
*Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D Yamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CogArena is a benchmark for evaluating LLM cognitive scores across 13 paradigms, testing whether these scores form stable, theory-aligned dimensions. Results show weak evidence for five-dimensional profiles: positive correlations exist, but within-group advantages are small and inconsistent; interventions show minimal matched-grouping effects, and out-of-family prediction fails. A post-hoc replication confirms weak effects. The study concludes that current evidence does not support stable cognitive profiles.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To determine if LLM cognitive scores can be reliably grouped into stable, theory-motivated dimensions that generalize beyond specific models, by testing convergence, intervention selectivity, and out-of-family prediction.

Method: CogArena uses a procedurally generated 13-paradigm benchmark with a multimethod framework. It analyzes correlations across paradigms, tests targeted scaffolds in a fully crossed design, evaluates in-battery diagonal effects, and conducts post-hoc replication with alternate wording.

Result: Most paradigm correlations are positive; a common axis explains ~50% of variance. Within-grouping advantages are small, scoring-sensitive, and inconsistent across model families. Scaffold-specific effects do not survive multiplicity correction. Out-of-family prediction fails. Post-hoc replication yields smaller positive estimates and also fails.

Conclusion: Theory-aligned prompting shows a minor in-battery diagonal tendency, but the evidence does not support stable five-dimensional cognitive profiles. CogArena provides a rigorous workflow for validating cognitive labels before assignment.

Abstract: LLM cognitive scores are increasingly summarized as per-ability profiles whose dimensions should converge across tasks, respond selectively to matched interventions, and generalize beyond the models used to define them. We introduce CogArena, a procedurally generated 13-paradigm benchmark built around a multimethod framework for determining when cognitive-task scores warrant dimensional labels across five theory-motivated groupings. Across 55 open-weight models, nearly all paradigm correlations are positive and a common axis explains about half the variance. The within-grouping advantage is small, scoring-sensitive, and uncertain across model families. In a separately frozen, fully crossed study across 12 models from six families, targeted scaffolds show a small matched-grouping advantage, but no scaffold-specific contrast survives multiplicity correction and selectivity does not improve held-out-family prediction. The frozen confirmation criterion fails. A post-hoc alternate-wording replication produces a smaller positive estimate and again fails. Together, these results support a boundary conclusion. Theory-aligned prompting produces a small in-battery diagonal tendency, but the present evidence does not establish stable five-dimensional profiles. CogArena provides a workflow joining behavioral signatures, covariance, matched interventions, and out-of-family prediction before cognitive labels are attached to model scores.

</details>


### [93] [DS@GT ARC at CheckThat! 2026: LLM-Based Trace Ranking and Grouped Reward Modeling for Multilingual Numerical Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25069)
*Sagnik Sinha,Shreyas Shrestha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于评估数值型声明的系统，针对英语和阿拉伯语，通过两种方法对大语言模型生成的推理轨迹进行排序并预测最终结论。第一种方法使用LoRA微调的LLM验证器对每个推理轨迹独立评分，并采用Best-of-N选择最终结论；第二种方法则采用轻量级TF-IDF奖励模型结合手工设计的数值与时间重叠特征进行评分。实验发现，基于LLM的方法在大多数指标上表现更优，尤其是在Recall@5方面；而基于奖励模型的方法在冲突类上表现更好。子声明分解未提升性能，表明拆分可能引入噪声。对于阿拉伯语，AraBERT优于通用多语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化验证数值型声明需要结合语言理解与定量推理，现有方法在处理复杂声明时存在局限性，尤其在多语言环境下。因此，亟需高效且准确的验证框架以支持跨语言的数值声明判断。

Method: 提出两种方法：一是利用LoRA微调的LLM验证器对推理轨迹进行二分类评分，并通过Best-of-N策略选取最终结论；二是采用基于TF-IDF的轻量级奖励模型，结合手工设计的数值与时间重叠特征进行评分，按结论类别聚合得分。同时对比了多语言模型与专用于阿拉伯语的AraBERT模型的效果。

Result: LLM-based方法在多数指标（尤其是Recall@5）上优于轻量级奖励模型；奖励模型在‘冲突’类别上表现更佳。子声明分解未提升性能，反而引入噪声。在阿拉伯语任务中，AraBERT显著优于通用多语言模型。

Conclusion: 基于微调的LLM验证器在数值声明验证中具有更强的整体性能，尤其适用于高召回率需求场景；而轻量级奖励模型在特定类别上仍有价值。子声明分解不推荐使用。对于阿拉伯语，使用领域特定预训练模型（如AraBERT）能显著提升效果。

Abstract: Automated verification of numerical claims is a challenging problem, as it requires both language understanding and quantitative reasoning. This paper describes our system for CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Task 2, which focuses on ranking reasoning traces generated by large language models (LLMs) and predicting a final verdict for numerical claims in English and Arabic. We explore two approaches. The first approach fine-tunes an LLM-based verifier using LoRA to score each reasoning trace independently as a binary classification problem, and selects the final verdict using Best-of-N selection. We further experiment with adaptive sub-claim decomposition to break complex claims into simpler parts before verification. The second approach uses a lightweight TF-IDF reward model with handcrafted numeric and temporal overlap features to score traces, and aggregates scores by verdict group to determine the final prediction. For Arabic, we compare a general multilingual model against AraBERT, a language-specific model pretrained on Arabic text. Our results show that the LLM-based approach outperforms the lightweight reward model on most metrics, particularly Recall@5, while the reward-based approach shows stronger performance on the Conflicting class. Sub-claim decomposition did not improve performance, suggesting that claim splitting introduces noise rather than aiding reasoning. For Arabic, AraBERT outperforms the multilingual baseline across most metrics.

</details>


### [94] [Evaluating Communicative Belief Updates in Large Language Models via Implicature Recognition and Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25094)
*Cesare Spinoso-Di Piano,Verna Dankers,Marius Mosbach,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（LLMs）识别隐含意义及其取消能力（即语用现象中，话语的暗示意义被削弱或否定）的能力。研究构建了首个专家标注的隐含意义取消数据集[DatasetName]，通过众包收集人类对隐含意义及其对应取消的判断。结果发现，与人类相比，当前LLMs在理解未言明信念及信念更新方面仍有差距，尤其在更自然的情境中表现较差。控制实验表明，LLMs的成功可能部分源于其先前信念的依赖，而失败则与其类型和形式有关。总体而言，当前LLMs尚未达到人类水平的未言明信念与信念更新理解能力。代码与数据已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语言受未言明信念及其更新驱动，因此准确建模这些认知过程对实现大语言模型与用户间的有效沟通至关重要。现有研究缺乏对LLMs在隐含意义及其取消理解方面的系统评估，亟需一个高质量、人工标注的数据集来检验其语用推理能力。

Method: 构建首个专家标注的隐含意义取消数据集[DatasetName]，通过众包方式收集人类对隐含意义及其取消的判断；将该数据集用于评估多个主流大语言模型在理解隐含意义及其取消任务上的表现，并设计控制实验分析模型成功与失败背后的原因。

Result: LLMs在理解隐含意义及其取消方面显著落后于人类，尤其是在自然语境中；模型的成功可能依赖于其先验信念，而失败则取决于隐含意义的类型与表达形式。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型尚未具备人类水平的未言明信念与信念更新理解能力，仍需进一步改进其语用推理机制。

Abstract: Human language is driven by unspoken beliefs and belief updates, making these critical to model for successful communication between large language models (LLMs) and their users. In this paper, we evaluate the ability of LLMs to recognize unspoken beliefs made through implicatures and to understand their updates through implicature cancellation: the pragmatic phenomenon whereby an utterance's implied meaning is weakened or negated. We create the first expert-annotated implicature cancellation dataset, [DatasetName], crowdsourced for human judgements of implicatures and their corresponding cancellations. We find that LLM belief update understanding lags behind that of humans, especially in more naturally-occurring scenarios. Additional control experiments suggest that successes in LLM belief updates may stem in part from a reliance on prior beliefs, and that failures in belief updates may depend on their type and on their form. Overall, our study suggests that current LLMs have not yet reached human-level understanding of unspoken beliefs and belief updates. Code and data are available at https://github.com/cesare-spinoso/ImplicatureX.

</details>


### [95] [Deep Label-Wise Attentive Temporal Convolutional Networks Improve Medical Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25129)
*Muhammed Yavuz Nuzumlalı,Alexander Fabbri,Irene Li,Dragomir Radev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将医疗编码建模为多标签文本分类问题，提出一种基于多层时间卷积网络（TCN）和标签特定注意力机制的深度神经模型。该模型能够有效捕捉长序列中的全局文档表示，并针对每个代码关注文本的不同部分。实验表明，该方法在F-1分数上比之前最优模型提升9%，召回率提升28%，尤其在临床决策支持场景中更具意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗编码需要从病历文本中整合多部分信息并针对不同代码关注不同内容，对人类编码员也极具挑战性。现有方法难以有效处理长序列依赖和标签间的差异性关注，因此亟需更高效、精准的自动化编码模型。

Method: 提出一种结合多层时间卷积网络（TCN）与标签特定注意力机制的深度神经模型。多层TCN用于提取长序列的全局上下文表示，标签特定注意力机制使模型能根据每个诊断或操作码聚焦文本的不同区域，实现细粒度的信息选择。

Result: 在标准数据集上，该模型的F-1分数相比前人最佳模型提升9%，召回率显著提高28%。更高的召回率表明模型能更全面地识别出应标注的编码，这对临床决策支持系统尤为重要。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型通过融合全局上下文建模与标签感知注意力，在医疗编码任务上实现了显著性能提升，尤其在提升召回率方面表现突出，具备较强的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Medical coding is the task of assigning a set of diagnosis and procedure codes for a hospitalization using recorded notes. It requires aggregating information from different parts of the text and focus to different sections for each individual code, making it a very difficult problem even for professional human coders. We model the task as a multi-label text classification problem. To overcome the mentioned difficulties, we propose a deep neural model consisting of a multi-layer temporal convolution network (TCN) followed by label-wise attention. While multi-layer TCN helps extract a global document representation with the ability to learn relations over very long sequences, label-specific attention mechanism allows the model to focus on different aspects of the same document for each individual label. Our method achieves significantly better F-1 scores (9% increase) compared to the previous state-of-the-art model, with a remarkable increase in recall score (28% increase), which we believe is the more important metric for a clinical decision support setting.

</details>


### [96] [TabRank: Chain-of-Thought Distillation for Table Re-Rankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25182)
*Adarsh Singh,Kushal Raj Bhandari,Jianxi Gao,Soham Dan,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabRank框架，用于训练用于表格检索的推理重排序模型。通过构建包含6728条推理轨迹的综合性数据集，并探索两种训练紧凑推理模型的方法：显式思维链（CoT）蒸馏和在提示中对教师推理轨迹进行条件化。在多种分布外泛化设置和多表场景下对TabRank进行压力测试，结果表明其在多个表格检索数据集上显著提升性能，相比基线模型在HybridQA、SQA、TabFact和TATQA子集上的Acc@10分别提升30.5%、15.2%、52.9%和13.1%。特别地，TabRank在多表推理任务中表现出良好的泛化能力。代码、数据和模型已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格检索系统依赖于重排序器来优化第一阶段检索器生成的候选列表，而传统的稀疏或密集检索模型在语义理解和推理能力上存在局限。尽管大型推理模型（LRMs）在非结构化段落检索中展现出优异的推理能力，但在表格检索中的应用仍不足。因此，亟需一种能够有效利用推理能力的重排序方法以提升表格检索质量。

Method: 提出TabRank框架，构建了基于Natural Questions Tables数据集的6728条推理轨迹数据集；采用两种训练策略：显式思维链蒸馏与将教师模型的推理轨迹作为提示输入至学生模型；通过多域、多表场景下的分布外测试验证模型泛化能力。

Result: TabRank在多个表格检索基准数据集上均取得显著性能提升，尤其在多表推理任务中表现突出。具体而言，在HybridQA、SQA、TabFact和TATQA子集上，Acc@10分别提升30.5%、15.2%、52.9%和13.1%，且具备良好的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: TabRank成功实现了基于推理的表格重排序，显著提升了表格检索性能，特别是在复杂多表场景下展现了强大的泛化能力，为未来结构化信息检索提供了新的思路和技术支持。

Abstract: The ability to retrieve relevant tables for answering questions is a key task for structured information retrieval. Multi-stage retrieval systems rely heavily on rerankers to refine candidate lists produced by efficient first-stage retrievers. As a result, neural rerankers and LLM-based reranking methods have become increasingly important due to their superior capacity for semantic understanding and reasoning compared to conventional sparse or dense retrieval models. Recently, Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) equipped with explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning have shown strong improvements in ranking quality in unstructured passage retrieval. In this work, we present TabRank, a framework for training reasoning rerankers for Tabular Retrieval. We first present a comprehensive dataset of 6728 reasoning traces for tabular reranking on the Natural Questions Tables dataset. We then explore two variants of training a compact reasoning model on these reasoning traces: explicit CoT distillation and conditioning the student reranker on the teacher's reasoning trace within the prompt. We stress-test TabRank on several out-of-distribution generalization settings on diverse domains and multi-table scenarios. Our approach significantly improves performance across a variety of table retrieval datasets, increasing Acc@10 by 30.5% on HybridQA, 15.2% on SQA, 52.9% on TabFact, and 13.1% on TATQA subsets of the Multi-Table QA Benchmark compared to the base model. Notably, TabRank generalizes effectively to multi-table reasoning. Our code, data and models are available at https://github.com/AdarshSingh7647/TabRanker

</details>


### [97] [A scaling law of contextual persistence in human language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25184)
*Elan Barenholtz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现人类语言中词语的排列顺序遵循一种类似幂律的规律，即上下文持续性函数P(d)随距离d衰减近似为1/d，表明语境影响在对数时间尺度上大致均匀分布。这一规律在多个语言家族和文本/口语模态中一致存在，且在随机打乱或合成数据中消失，基因组和蛋白质序列中亦未观察到。研究使用大语言模型作为概率探针，揭示了语言结构中深层次的统计规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人类语言中词语排列顺序是否具有可量化的、跨语言的统计规律，以理解语言如何通过上下文传递意义。

Method: 利用大规模语言模型作为概率探针，计算目标词在真实语序与随机打乱语序下的困惑度差异，从而定义上下文持续性函数P(d)，并分析其随距离的衰减模式。

Result: P(d)在十种不同语料库中均呈现约1/d的衰减趋势（平均α=1.04，中位数决定系数r²=0.96），且在控制条件下不出现，仅在自然语言中显现。

Conclusion: 人类语言中词语的排列顺序遵循一个普遍存在的尺度律——上下文持续性，其指数接近1，表明语义影响在对数时间尺度上均匀分布，揭示了语言深层结构的统一性。

Abstract: Human language exhibits lawful structure at the level of words (frequency, vocabulary growth) and word pairs (co-occurrence across distance). Here we show that the arrangement of words in sequence -- a central determinant of meaning -- obeys a comparable law. Using large language models as probabilistic probes, we measured the reduction in target perplexity conferred by prior context at distance d beyond that of the same words scrambled; this difference, the contextual persistence function P(d), isolates the influence of arrangement. Across ten corpora spanning six language families and written and spoken modalities, P(d) decayed approximately as 1/d ($P(d) \propto d^{-α}$, mean $α= 1.04$; median $r^2 = 0.96$). The effect vanished in scrambled and synthetic controls, replicated across independent probes, and did not appear in genomic or protein sequences under domain-native models. An exponent near 1 distributes contextual influence approximately uniformly across logarithmic timescales. The results establish a scaling law of contextual persistence in human language.

</details>


### [98] [MyoCardBench: A Real-World Data Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models in Clinically Authentic Cardiovascular Care Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25186)
*Xiao Li,Mouxiao Bian,Zhaodi Wu,Sijie Ren,Juechen Chen,Lu Lu,Jingru Ding,Yun Zhong,Jie Xu,Yixiu Liang,Junbo Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MyoCardBench 是首个覆盖心血管诊疗全流程的大型真实世界基准，包含2,263个来自临床记录与考试数据的任务，由16名心脏病专家标注并经两位资深医生复核。7个大语言模型在零样本设置下生成15,841条输出，评估涵盖关键点覆盖率、整体临床质量及伦理准确性。GPT-5.4表现最佳（宏观平均62.55，加权均值62.19），但所有模型在CardioECGRead和CardioEthics任务中表现较差，且整体临床质量与关键点覆盖率差距显著，尤其在CardioComm、CardioEmergRescue和CardioTreatPlan中。该基准为评估和改进医疗LLM提供了全面框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型评测多集中于单一考试知识或孤立任务，无法反映心血管疾病诊疗中纵向性、多模态及高安全性要求的真实临床流程。因此亟需一个覆盖完整诊疗周期、贴近真实临床场景的综合性评测基准。

Method: 构建包含13个任务子集的MyoCardBench，基于去标识化心血管病历与考试数据；由16名心脏病专家进行标注与参考答案构建，并经两名高级心脏科医师交叉审核；采用标准化零样本设置，让7个大语言模型生成输出；通过关键点覆盖率、整体临床质量评分（针对开放任务）及准确率（CardioEthics）进行评估。

Result: GPT-5.4在宏观平均（62.55）和加权均值（62.19）上表现最优，且在所有维度排名第一。各任务中，CardioAuxReport得分最高（86.38），而CardioECGRead（17.25）和CardioEthics（17.34）最低。关键点覆盖率与整体临床质量之间的最大差距出现在CardioComm（52.71）、CardioEmergRescue（52.05）和CardioTreatPlan（48.80）。

Conclusion: MyoCardBench是目前最大规模、最全面的真实世界多任务基准，首次系统覆盖心血管诊疗全周期，提供最广泛临床真实场景，可有效识别模型优势、关键遗漏及未来优化方向，具有重要研究价值与实践意义。

Abstract: Background: Most medical large language model (LLM) benchmarks focus on examination knowledge or isolated tasks and may not reflect the longitudinal, multimodal, and safety-critical workflow of cardiovascular care. Objective: To develop MyoCardBench, a real-world benchmark spanning the cardiovascular care continuum, and assess LLM performance across clinical dimensions and specialist tasks. Methods: MyoCardBench includes 2,263 items from 13 task-specific datasets derived from de-identified cardiovascular records and examination data. Sixteen cardiology physicians conducted annotation and reference construction, followed by cross-review from two senior cardiologists. Seven LLMs generated 15,841 outputs under standardized zero-shot settings. Open-ended tasks were evaluated using key-point coverage and holistic clinical quality, while CardioEthics was scored by accuracy. Results: GPT-5.4 achieved the highest macro-average (62.55) and item-weighted mean (62.19), followed by Gemini 3.1 Pro (59.95) and Qwen 3.6 27B (59.72). GPT-5.4 ranked first in all three dimensions. CardioAuxReport performed best (86.38), whereas CardioECGRead (17.25) and CardioEthics (17.34) were lowest. The largest gaps between holistic clinical quality and key-point coverage occurred in CardioComm (52.71), CardioEmergRescue (52.05), and CardioTreatPlan (48.80). Conclusions: To our knowledge, MyoCardBench is the largest real-world, multi-task benchmark for LLM evaluation across the cardiovascular care continuum and offers the broadest coverage of clinically authentic cardiology scenarios reported to date. It provides a rigorous framework for identifying model strengths, clinically important omissions, and priorities for future development.

</details>


### [99] [A Cross-lingual Comparison of Human and Classification Model Entrainment Behavior in Code-switched Speech Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25202)
*Debasmita Bhattacharya,Siying Ding,Alayna Nguyen,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在汉语-英语、印地语-英语和西班牙语-英语对话中跨语言的对话同化现象，发现虽然词汇层面的同化具有普遍性，但声学-韵律和代码转换风格层面的同化表现出情境特异性。通过分类模型分析，发现经典与Transformer模型虽能较好识别同化行为，但其关注的特征并非人类行为中最显著的特征，提示当前模型在多语言风格建模上仍存在不足。研究提出一种以人类行为为基础的评估框架，为构建自然代码转换对话代理提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对单语和书面语境下的对话同化有较多探讨，但在口语代码转换场景中的研究仍不足。理解不同语言对之间的同化模式，有助于提升多语言对话系统的自然性和适应性。

Method: 采用跨语言分析方法，研究三种语言对（汉语-英语、印地语-英语、西班牙语-英语）的对话同化行为；结合分类模型（包括经典与Transformer模型），利用特征重要性和消融分析，评估模型对同化特征的捕捉能力。

Result: 词汇同化在不同语言对间具有一致性，而声学-韵律及代码转换风格的同化表现出显著的情境差异；分类模型虽能有效检测同化，但其决策依据的特征与人类最关注的特征不一致。

Conclusion: 当前的机器学习模型未能准确反映人类在多语言对话中的真实同化行为，需发展更符合人类行为模式的评估框架，以推动自然代码转换对话系统的发展。

Abstract: Conversational entrainment is well-studied in monolingual and written contexts, but remains underexplored in spoken code-switching (CSW). We present a novel cross-lingual analysis of entrainment in Mandarin-English, Hindi-English, and Spanish-English dialogue and show that, while lexical entrainment generalizes across language pairs, entrainment over acoustic-prosodic and CSW style aspects exhibits context-specific variation. We build on these findings by asking whether classification models capture these human behavioral patterns. Applying feature importance and ablation analyses, we find that classical and Transformer-based classifiers detect entrainment reasonably well but consistently prioritize features other than those most salient to human entraining behavior. Our approach introduces a human-grounded framework for evaluating model decision-making in multilingual stylistic contexts, and suggests future challenges for developing conversational agents capable of producing naturalistic code-switched speech.

</details>
